# WolframInstitute/PureMath paclet modules: existing Wolfram Language functions for the Wolfram Physics Project

Research report for the WolframInstitute/WolframPhysics guide page. Source swept: the WolframInstitute/PureMath paclet, read from three places: the GitHub repository https://github.com/WolframInstitute/PureMath (default branch main, PacletInfo version 0.5.0, head commit 3df8abea of 2026-09-16, 205 kernel module files under PureMath/Kernel, 2773 exported symbols), the locally installed paclet version 0.4.5 (PacletFind, 189 kernel modules, 2527 exported symbols, context `` WolframInstitute`PureMath` ``, WL 15.0+), and the nikm Wolfram Cloud deployed resource https://www.wolframcloud.com/obj/nikm/DeployedResources/Paclet/WolframInstitute/PureMath (535 documented symbols listed; the page shows no version number). Date of sweep: 17 September 2026. Kernel: WL 15.0 via wolframscript, cloud-connected as nikm.

Earlier reports (docs/research/github-and-source-code.md sections A and C, docs/research/wolfram-institute.md table 14) catalogued Foundations/HypergraphRewriting.wl, TypedHypergraphRewriting.wl, RewritingSystems.wl, AlgebraicRewriting.wl, CausalSets.wl, MathematicalPhysics/CausalSetFieldTheory.wl, a 48-row MathematicalPhysics subset, DiscreteMathematics/GraphCurvature.wl and PathReconvergence.wl. This report sweeps every other kernel module, gives one table per module judged relevant (with every exported symbol and the first sentence of its usage message), one row per module judged adjacent (symbol count and example names, so nothing is dropped silently), and re-lists the earlier modules symbol by symbol with the 0.4.5 kernel confirmation that the earlier reports lacked. Every row was seen in a fetched module file and, unless the Status column says otherwise, confirmed by Names and a usage string in the installed 0.4.5 kernel.

## Method

Every URL, command and kernel evaluation below was actually run. Scratch files live under the session scratchpad directory (puremath/ subdirectory); the kernel scripts are named kernel1.wls to kernel7.wls.

### GitHub (gh CLI, authenticated)

- `gh api "repos/WolframInstitute/PureMath/git/trees/main?recursive=1"` (4216 tree entries, not truncated), filtered to every .wl file under PureMath/Kernel (205 files) and every .md file under docs/en/Guides (156 files).
- `gh api repos/WolframInstitute/PureMath` (default branch main) and `gh api "repos/WolframInstitute/PureMath/commits?per_page=3"` (head 3df8abea, 2026-09-16T03:32:17Z, "Merge pull request #190 from WolframInstitute/feature/pre-review-alg-geom").
- `gh api -H "Accept: application/vnd.github.raw" repos/WolframInstitute/PureMath/contents/PureMath/PacletInfo.wl` (Version 0.5.0, WolframVersion 15.0+).
- `gh api -H "Accept: application/vnd.github.raw" repos/WolframInstitute/PureMath/contents/PureMath/Kernel/<Area>/<Module>.wl` for all 205 kernel files (raw.githubusercontent.com returns 404 for this repository). The complete list of fetched module paths is in the inventory table below; every module row and every symbol row in this report points at the blob URL https://github.com/WolframInstitute/PureMath/blob/main/PureMath/Kernel/<Area>/<Module>.wl of the file it was read from.
- The same endpoint for all 156 files under docs/en/Guides (read for the guide groupings in the Findings section).
- A Python extractor (extract.py) over the 205 files collected every `PackageExported[...]` name (both the one-name form and the list form), every `Name::usage = "..."` string (regular expression over the raw file, whitespace normalized), every `Options[Name] = {...}` key list and every `SyntaxInformation[Name] = {...}` line. Totals: 2773 exported symbols, 2773 usage messages for exported symbols (none missing), 118 Options definitions, 0 SyntaxInformation definitions. Twelve modules also define usage messages for file-private helpers (for example GAPLink.wl and Geometry/HyperbolicGeometry.wl); those helpers are not exported and are not listed.

### Cloud

- WebFetch and `curl -sL` of https://www.wolframcloud.com/obj/nikm/DeployedResources/Paclet/WolframInstitute/PureMath (223 KB HTML; the Symbols list of 535 names, the guide navigation, the installation command `PacletInstall[ResourceObject["https://wolfr.am/1EYpNwe4m"]]`, and the links to https://github.com/WolframInstitute/PureMath and to PureMath-ResourceDefinition.nb and PureMath-ExampleNotebook.nb under https://www.wolframcloud.com/download/nikm/DeployedResources/Paclet/WolframInstitute/PureMath/). `curl -sI https://wolfr.am/1EYpNwe4m` (301 to the same cloud object). The page carries no version string; the per-symbol pages under Documentation/ref/<Name>.html render the same landing page (HasseDiagram.html checked).
- `curl -sL` of https://www.wolframcloud.com/download/nikm/DeployedResources/Paclet/WolframInstitute/PureMath/PureMath-ResourceDefinition.nb to read the deployed version: the transfer stopped at 2,574,653 of the 4,584,310 bytes the notebook header declares, and the partial text carries no version string (see Gaps).
- WebFetch of https://resources.wolframcloud.com/FunctionRepository/resources/MyrheimMeyerDimension/ (302 to a 401 login page) and WebSearch "MyrheimMeyerDimension" Wolfram Function Repository (no hit), to check a clash the brief asserted.

### Kernel evaluations (wolframscript -file, WL 15.0)

- kernel1.wls, kernel2.wls: `PacletFind["WolframInstitute/PureMath"]` (versions 0.4.5 at ~/Library/Wolfram/Paclets/Repository/WolframInstitute__PureMath-0.4.5 and 0.0.2), `` Needs["WolframInstitute`PureMath`"] `` (loads in about three seconds), `` Names["WolframInstitute`PureMath`*"] `` (2527 names), `` Names["WolframInstitute`PureMath`PackageScope`*"] `` (60 names), the values of the version and categories variables (Version 0.4.5, WolframVersion 15.0+, Author "PureMath contributors"; categories Algebra, Analysis, AppliedMathematics, DifferentialEquations, DiscreteMathematics, Foundations, Geometry, MathematicalPhysics, NumberTheory, ProbabilityAndStatistics, Topology), and export of every usage string (all 2527 names carry one) to kernel_names_0.4.5.json.
- A Python diff of the GitHub extraction against the kernel export: 0 symbols in 0.4.5 that are absent from main; 246 symbols on main absent from 0.4.5 (in 30 modules, 16 of them whole modules that do not exist in 0.4.5); the 189 local kernel files were listed with `find` and compared with the 205 on main.
- kernel3.wls: `PacletFind["*"]` filtered to physics-project paclets (Wolfram/Lambda 1.2.4, WolframInstitute/Infrageometry 1.0.14, WolframInstitute/SyntheticInfrageometry 0.12.36, SetReplace 0.3.196, Wolfram/Multicomputation 0.1.9, WolframInstitute/Hypergraph 1.2.3, WolframInstitute/TuringMachine 1.2.0, WolframInstitute/HypergraphRewriteEngine 0.0.1, Wolfram/Metamathematics 1.0.2, RichardAssar/TMProofs 1.0.0).
- kernel4.wls: name clashes computed mechanically: `Needs` of each of `` Wolfram`Lambda` ``, `` WolframInstitute`Infrageometry` ``, `` WolframInstitute`SyntheticInfrageometry` ``, `` SetReplace` ``, `` Wolfram`Multicomputation` ``, `` WolframInstitute`Hypergraph` ``, `` WolframInstitute`TuringMachine` `` and `` Wolfram`QuantumFramework` `` (2.1.1) followed by `Intersection` of their `Names[ctx <> "*"]` with the 2773 PureMath names, and `` NameQ["System`" <> name] `` for every PureMath name (no System clash). Wolfram/Categorica is not installed, so its clash set was not computed.
- kernel5.wls: `ResourceObject[name]["ResourceType"]` for 154 candidate names (150 of them PureMath exports) (each wrapped in TimeConstrained 40 s) to find Function Repository clashes; 15 hits (listed in the clash table).
- kernel6.wls and kernel7.wls (verification): a fresh kernel, `Needs`, then for each of the 948 row names `` NameQ["WolframInstitute`PureMath`" <> name] `` and `StringQ[ToExpression[ctx <> name <> "::usage"]]`; `PacletFindRemote["WolframInstitute/PureMath"]` (returns {}: the paclet is not in the public Paclet Repository); spot evaluations BetaNormalForm[LambdaApply[LambdaAbstraction[x, LambdaVariable[x]], LambdaVariable[y]]] (LambdaVariable[y]), ChurchNumeral[2] (a nested LambdaAbstraction), Head[HasseDiagram[Poset[{1, 2, 3, 6}, Divisible[#2, #1] &]]] (Graph). Two attempts to evaluate `ResourceObject["https://wolfr.am/1EYpNwe4m"]` (kernel6.wls with TimeConstrained 240 s, kernel8.wls with 580 s) started a 3.88 MB download; the first was cut off by the time limit and the second ended in URLFetch::invhttp, libcurl error 18, end of response with 666796 bytes missing. PacletFind afterwards still reports only 0.4.5 and 0.0.2, so nothing was installed.
- Re-fetch of five module files at the end of the sweep (ProofTheory.wl, OrderTheory.wl, SpacetimeObservables.wl, ComputationalMechanics.wl, DiscreteExteriorCalculus.wl) with the same gh api endpoint: SHA-256 identical to the first fetch; head commit unchanged (3df8abea).

### Guides read

docs/en/Guides/PureMath.md; Foundations/{Foundations, ProofTheory, RewritingSystems, CausalSets, OrderTheory, UniversalAlgebra, MathematicalLogic, ModelTheory, CategoryTheory, TypeTheory, LatticeTheory, SetTheory, AxiomaticSetTheory, ModalLogic, DescriptiveSetTheory, CombinatorialSetTheory}.md; DiscreteMathematics/{BooleanFunctions, Combinatorics, DesignTheory, DiscreteMathematics, EnumerativeCombinatorics, GraphCurvature, GraphPolynomials, GraphTheory, GraphZetaFunctions, PathReconvergence}.md; Topology/{AlgebraicTopology, DifferentialTopology, DiscreteExteriorCalculus, DiscreteMorseTheory, GeneralTopology, KTheory, KnotTheory, SimplicialSets, TopologicalQuantumFieldTheory, Topology}.md; MathematicalPhysics/{MathematicalPhysics, GeneralRelativity, CausalSetFieldTheory, SpecialRelativity, StatisticalMechanics, DynamicalSystems, ChaosFractals}.md and QuantumTheory/{QuantumTheory, QuantumMechanics, QuantumFieldTheory}.md; Geometry/{Geometry, DiscreteGeometry, CombinatorialGeometry, ComputationalGeometry, HyperbolicGeometry, InteriorGeometry, HodgeTheory}.md and DifferentialGeometry/{DifferentialGeometry, Curvature, Connections, GaugeTheory, Manifolds}.md; ProbabilityAndStatistics/{ComputationalMechanics, ProcessRealization, StochasticProcesses}.md; AppliedMathematics/{InformationTheory, GameTheory}.md. The other guides were downloaded and parsed for section headings only.

## Findings

Column conventions: Name is the exact symbol spelling (a dollar-prefixed name is set in a code span); Source is the paclet and the kernel module file; URL is the GitHub blob URL of that module on main; Description is the first sentence of the usage message in the words of the source (long sentences are cut at a word boundary with an ellipsis; a pipe or a dollar-prefixed name inside a message is rewritten so the table survives); Topics gives the project topic, then any name clash found mechanically (clash: Wolfram/Lambda, clash: WolframInstitute/Infrageometry, clash: WolframInstitute/SyntheticInfrageometry, clash: Wolfram/QuantumFramework, clash: Function Repository); Status is "seen" when the symbol is exported on GitHub main and present with a usage string in the installed 0.4.5 kernel, and "seen; GitHub main only, not in 0.4.5" when it is exported on main but the 0.4.5 kernel does not know it.

### 1. Foundations: proof theory, lambda calculus and combinators (ProofTheory.wl)

Foundations/ProofTheory.wl: 49 exported symbols on main, 49 of them in 0.4.5. Module header: The computational core of proof theory and the simply typed lambda calculus, treating proofs and programs as formal syntactic objects.

### 2. Foundations: machine-checked proofs (FormalProof.wl)

### 3. Foundations: mathematical logic and modal logic (MathematicalLogic.wl, ModalLogic.wl)

Foundations/MathematicalLogic.wl: 23 exported symbols on main, 23 of them in 0.4.5. Module header: The metalogical layer the kernel's Boolean engine lacks: literal/connective analysis, an equisatisfiable CNF (Tseitin) transform, first-order syntax (free/bound variables, capture-avoiding substitution, closures, alpha equivalence, well-formedness over a signature), the first-order normal forms (prenex, Skolem, clausal), and a little proof-theoretic metalogic (sequents, unique existence, .

Foundations/ModelTheory.wl: 30 exported symbols on main, 30 of them in 0.4.5. ### 8. Foundations: category theory (CategoryTheory.wl, AbstractCategory.wl, AbstractFunctor.wl, UniversalConstructions.wl, StringDiagrams.wl, ToposTheory.wl, SheafTheory.wl)

Foundations/CategoryTheory.wl: 14 exported symbols on main, 14 of them in 0.4.5. Module header: A small CONCRETE, computational category-theory layer: categories whose objects are real Manifolds / Rings and whose morphisms actually compute, the functors PureMath already has (the tangent functor = Pushforward, the cotangent / pullback functor = Pullback, ring-change induced by a RingHomomorphism), and the laws that make them checkable (FunctorialityQ, NaturalityQ).

### 9. Foundations: axiomatic set theory (AxiomaticSetTheory.wl)

### 21. Name clashes found mechanically

Computed in kernel4.wls and kernel5.wls (see Method). A clash means the same short symbol name is exported by both paclets, so loading both puts the name on the context path twice and the later Needs wins unless the name is written with its context. SetReplace, Wolfram/Multicomputation, WolframInstitute/Hypergraph and WolframInstitute/TuringMachine share no name with PureMath (their relation is by topic, noted in the Topics column of sections 10 and 11).

| Clashing paclet or repository | PureMath names | Where the PureMath name lives |
|---|---|---|
| Wolfram/Lambda | BetaReduce, ChurchNumeral, EtaReduce, LambdaFreeVariables | Foundations/ProofTheory.wl |
| WolframInstitute/Infrageometry | AlexandrovTopology, BarycentricRefinement, ContinuousMapQ, DeltaComplexQ, DiracHodgeMatrix, DominationNumber, FormanRicciCurvature, GraphComplex, HodgeLaplacianMatrix, OllivierRicciCurvature, SimplexList, SimplexStar, SimplicialMap, SimplicialSet, SimplicialSetQ | DiscreteMathematics/GraphCurvature.wl, DiscreteMathematics/GraphTheory.wl, Topology/AlgebraicTopology.wl, Topology/DiscreteExteriorCalculus.wl, Topology/GeneralTopology.wl, Topology/SimplicialSets.wl |
| WolframInstitute/SyntheticInfrageometry | MetricInterval | Geometry/InteriorGeometry.wl |
| Wolfram/QuantumFramework | QuantumOperator, QuantumState | MathematicalPhysics/QuantumMechanics.wl |
| Function Repository (ResourceObject lookup of 154 candidate names) | HasseDiagram, AbstractCategory, AbstractFunctor, BlockEntropy, PosetQ, Pfaffian, SpanningTreeCount, BettiNumbers, RiemannTensor, RicciTensor, EinsteinTensor, MetricTensor, HyperbolicDistance | Algebra/LinearAlgebra.wl, DiscreteMathematics/GraphTheory.wl, Foundations/AbstractCategory.wl, Foundations/AbstractFunctor.wl, Foundations/OrderTheory.wl, Geometry/Curvature.wl, Geometry/HyperbolicGeometry.wl, Geometry/ManifoldTheory.wl, ProbabilityAndStatistics/ComputationalMechanics.wl, Topology/AlgebraicTopology.wl |
| Function Repository, asserted by the brief but not found | MyrheimMeyerDimension | Foundations/CausalSets.wl; ResourceObject["MyrheimMeyerDimension"] returned no resource on 2026-09-17 and the resource page URL redirects to a login page, so this clash is unconfirmed |
| Wolfram/Categorica (not installed, not computed) | AbstractCategory, AbstractFunctor, AbstractProduct, AbstractCoproduct, AbstractPullback, AbstractPushout, AbstractLimit, AbstractNaturalTransformation, AbstractQuiver are described in their module headers as ports of Jonathan Gorard's Categorica design (the Function Repository AbstractCategory and AbstractFunctor entries are confirmed above) | Foundations/AbstractCategory.wl, AbstractFunctor.wl, UniversalConstructions.wl |

### 22. Guide groupings (docs/en/Guides on main)

The paclet documentation groups the symbols above as follows; only the guides for the areas in this report are listed, with their section headings and the number of PureMath symbols each section names (WL built-ins named in the guides are excluded). The guides also name the built-ins they build on (for example TransitiveClosureGraph, TopologicalSort, KendallTau and RandomPointConfiguration in the Causal Sets guide, StringReplaceList and SubstitutionSystem in the Rewriting Systems guide, CellularAutomaton and SubstitutionSystem in the Dynamical Systems guide).

## Gaps

- The deployed cloud version could not be read: the cloud page shows no version, PacletFindRemote returns nothing (the paclet is not in the public Paclet Repository), ResourceObject on the wolfr.am link starts a 3.88 MB download that was cut off once by the time limit and once by a libcurl end-of-response error, and the curl download of PureMath-ResourceDefinition.nb stopped at 2.57 of 4.58 MB. The installed 0.4.5 is the reference kernel; main is 0.5.0; the cloud page lists 535 documented symbols, all but ten of which are in both.
- 111 of the 948 symbol rows exist only on GitHub main (16 whole modules, among them SpacetimeObservables.wl, TopologicalQuantumFieldTheory.wl, SheafTheory.wl, AxiomaticSetTheory.wl, ModalLogic.wl, CrossSections.wl, OneLoopRenormalization.wl, ExternalStates.wl, plus parts of QuantumMechanics.wl, DynamicalSystems.wl, HybridClassicalQuantum.wl, ToposTheory.wl, RepresentationTheory.wl and others); these were seen in the fetched files but could not be evaluated in the installed kernel. Installing 0.5.0 from GitHub was out of scope.
- Wolfram/Categorica is not installed locally and its Paclet Repository page was not fetched, so the AbstractCategory family clash is inferred from the module headers, not computed.
- The Function Repository clash check covered 154 candidate names chosen by hand, not all 2773 names; a systematic ResourceSearch was not run (each call takes minutes).
- Usage messages, not reference pages, are the source of every description; the per-symbol reference pages on the cloud (Documentation/ref/<Name>.html) render the landing page, and the paclet's docs/en/ReferencePages were not read.
- Options keys were extracted by regular expression from `Options[Name] = {...}` blocks; option lists defined by Join or by reference to another symbol are reported as the symbol name only.
- The adjacent modules (section 19) are summarized by count and three example names; their full symbol lists are in extract.json in the scratchpad and in the module files linked.

## Verification

- Fresh kernel (kernel7.wls, 2026-09-17 01:59): Needs of the installed 0.4.5 succeeded (2527 names in context); of the 948 symbol rows in sections 1 to 18, 837 names satisfy NameQ and carry a String usage message; the 111 names that fail are exactly the set the GitHub-versus-kernel diff marks as main-only, and every such row says so in its Status column. No row name was found absent unexpectedly.
- Spot evaluations in the same kernel: BetaNormalForm of an applied identity abstraction returned LambdaVariable[y]; ChurchNumeral[2] returned a two-level LambdaAbstraction; HasseDiagram of the divisor poset of 6 returned a Graph.
- Re-fetch of five module files (ProofTheory.wl, OrderTheory.wl, SpacetimeObservables.wl, ComputationalMechanics.wl, DiscreteExteriorCalculus.wl) through the same gh api endpoint at the end of the sweep: SHA-256 identical to the first fetch; the head commit was still 3df8abea.
- Every symbol name in every table was produced by the extractor from a `PackageExported` declaration in the fetched module file; the description column was produced from the `::usage` string in the same file (the 0.4.5 kernel usage string was used only where the file had none, which did not occur). No name was typed by hand except the ten cloud-page names in section 20, which were copied from the parsed cloud page and are marked unconfirmed.
- Name clashes were computed by Intersection in the kernel, not by recollection; the one clash asserted by the brief that the kernel could not confirm (MyrheimMeyerDimension in the Function Repository) is marked unconfirmed in section 21.

Row counts: 948 symbol rows in sections 1 to 18 (837 seen in 0.4.5, 111 main-only), 138 adjacent module rows in section 19, 10 unconfirmed cloud-page rows in section 20.

### Independent verification pass (2026-09-16)

A second agent re-checked every row of every table above against the sources, without reusing the first sweep's extraction. Scratch files are under the session scratchpad directory (verify/ subdirectory: extract2.py, check_gh.py, check_status.py, check_inventory.py, check_s19.py, check_guides2.py, kernelA.wls to kernelD.wls).

- GitHub: the tree of main was fetched again (`gh api`, 4216 entries, head commit still 3df8abea) and all 205 kernel module files and the 45 guide files named in section 22 were re-downloaded with the authenticated raw-content endpoint. A fresh extractor over the module files found 2773 `PackageExported` names, 2773 usage messages, and the same per-area module and symbol counts as the inventory table in section 0. The repository is private, so the blob URLs in the URL column cannot be read by an unauthenticated fetch; every URL was verified through the gh api instead.
- Sections 1 to 18 (948 symbol rows): for every row the Name is declared `PackageExported` in the module given in the Source column, the URL is the blob URL of that module, the module carries a usage message for the name, and the Description column is a prefix of that usage message after the pipe and dollar rewrites described in the column conventions. Together the 67 module tables list every exported symbol of their modules, with none missing and none extra.
- Installed kernel (WL 15.0, PureMath 0.4.5 at ~/Library/Wolfram/Paclets/Repository/WolframInstitute__PureMath-0.4.5): `Needs` gives 2527 names; of the 948 row names, 837 satisfy `NameQ` with a String usage message and 111 do not, and the Status column agrees with the kernel row for row. The 189 local module files and the 246 main-only symbols by module match the lists in section 0.
- Section 19 (138 module rows): every module exists on main, the counts on main and in 0.4.5 match the kernel and the extraction, every example name is exported by the module named, and every quoted header sentence appears in the file. One row corrected: GAPLink.wl is one of the 16 modules absent from 0.4.5, so its Status is now "seen; GitHub main only, not in 0.4.5" instead of "seen", and its "Header:" text, which was a garbled extraction of the file's PackageScoped lines, now says the file has no header sentence.
- Section 20 (10 rows): the cloud page was fetched again (curl, 223 KB, 535 reference links; WebFetch confirmed Group, RingQ, VacuumQ, BifurcationDiagramData and HasseDiagram on the Symbols list and no version number); exactly these ten names are absent from main, the other 525 are on main and in 0.4.5, every "nearest current name" is exported in the module stated, and the 0.0.2 PacletInfo lists LogicAxiomSchemas with the dollar prefix. All ten stay unconfirmed.
- Section 21: the paclet clash sets were recomputed by `Intersection` in a fresh kernel after `Needs` of Wolfram/Lambda 1.2.4, WolframInstitute/Infrageometry 1.0.14, WolframInstitute/SyntheticInfrageometry 0.12.36, SetReplace 0.3.196, Wolfram/Multicomputation 0.1.9, WolframInstitute/Hypergraph 1.2.3, WolframInstitute/TuringMachine 1.2.0 and Wolfram/QuantumFramework 2.1.1, and again from the PacletInfo symbol lists of Lambda and QuantumFramework; the results are identical to the table, and no PureMath name is a System symbol. `ResourceObject` (TimeConstrained 60 s per name) returned a Function resource for all 13 Function Repository names; WebFetch of the resource pages for HasseDiagram, AbstractCategory, BlockEntropy, EinsteinTensor and HyperbolicDistance confirmed the names and contributors. `ResourceObject["MyrheimMeyerDimension"]` failed and the resource URL redirects to a 401 login page, so that row stays unconfirmed. The three module headers named in the Categorica row do describe the symbols as ports of Jonathan Gorard's Categorica. `PacletFindRemote` returns {} for both WolframInstitute/PureMath and Wolfram/Categorica.
- Section 22 (45 guide rows): every guide file exists on main, the frontmatter Description matches the row, and the section titles and their order match the file. The symbol counts were recomputed under the rule the column states (distinct PureMath exports named in the section, whether bare or in a call form, WL built-ins excluded); 16 counts in 7 rows had counted WL built-ins or repeated spans and were corrected: ProofTheory, Machine-checked proofs 4 to 3; CategoryTheory, Abstract categories 7 to 5, Toposes and presheaves 17 to 16, Universal constructions 19 to 11, String diagrams 9 to 5; RewritingSystems, One-step rewriting and normal forms 5 to 1, Hypergraph morphisms and the double-pushout step 34 to 29, Typed states, slices, and molecules 18 to 17, Linear-algebraic abstraction 8 to 6, Rewriting in other settings 6 to 4; GraphPolynomials, Kirchhoff and forest polynomials 7 to 6, Dimers 8 to 7; Curvature, Curves 3 to 0 (the section names only the built-ins FrenetSerretSystem, ArcCurvature and ArcLength); GaugeTheory, Gauge connections and field strength 5 to 4, Spin geometry and the Dirac operator 7 to 6; GeneralRelativity, Black Holes and Horizons 10 to 8. The other 32 section-count entries in those rows and all counts in the remaining 38 rows were reproduced exactly.
- Rows removed: none; no row names a function that exists nowhere. Names respelled or re-sourced: none. Status changes: the one GAPLink.wl row above. The 10 rows of section 20 and the MyrheimMeyerDimension row of section 21 remain the only unconfirmed entries.
